734:## Schema Commands
735:
736:Commands for creating and managing custom workflow schemas.
737:
738:### `openspec schema init`
739:
740:Create a new project-local schema.
741:
742:```
743:openspec schema init <name> [options]
744:```
745:
746:**Arguments:**
747:
748:| Argument | Required | Description |
749:|----------|----------|-------------|
750:| `name` | Yes | Schema name (kebab-case) |
751:
752:**Options:**
753:
754:| Option | Description |
755:|--------|-------------|
756:| `--description <text>` | Schema description |
757:| `--artifacts <list>` | Comma-separated artifact IDs (default: `proposal,specs,design,tasks`) |
758:| `--default` | Set as project default schema |
759:| `--no-default` | Don't prompt to set as default |
760:| `--force` | Overwrite existing schema |
761:| `--json` | Output as JSON |
762:
763:**Examples:**
764:
765:```bash
766:# Interactive schema creation
767:openspec schema init research-first
768:
769:# Non-interactive with specific artifacts
770:openspec schema init rapid \
771:  --description "Rapid iteration workflow" \
772:  --artifacts "proposal,tasks" \
773:  --default
774:```
775:
776:**What it creates:**
777:
778:```
779:openspec/schemas/<name>/
780:├── schema.yaml           # Schema definition
781:└── templates/
782:    ├── proposal.md       # Template for each artifact
783:    ├── specs.md
784:    ├── design.md
785:    └── tasks.md
786:```

896:
897:## Configuration Commands
898:
899:### `openspec config`
900:
901:View and modify global OpenSpec configuration.
902:
903:```
904:openspec config <subcommand> [options]
905:```
906:
907:**Subcommands:**
908:
909:| Subcommand | Description |
910:|------------|-------------|
911:| `path` | Show config file location |
912:| `list` | Show all current settings |
913:| `get <key>` | Get a specific value |
914:| `set <key> <value>` | Set a value |
915:| `unset <key>` | Remove a key |
916:| `reset` | Reset to defaults |
917:| `edit` | Open in `$EDITOR` |
918:| `profile [preset]` | Configure workflow profile interactively or via preset |
919:
920:**Examples:**
921:
922:```bash
923:# Show config file path
924:openspec config path
925:
926:# List all settings
927:openspec config list
928:
929:# Get a specific value
930:openspec config get telemetry.enabled
931:
932:# Set a value
933:openspec config set telemetry.enabled false
934:
935:# Set a string value explicitly
936:openspec config set user.name "My Name" --string
937:
938:# Remove a custom setting
939:openspec config unset user.name
940:
941:# Reset all configuration
942:openspec config reset --all --yes
943:
944:# Edit config in your editor
945:openspec config edit
946:
947:# Configure profile with action-based wizard
948:openspec config profile
949:
950:# Fast preset: switch workflows to core (keeps delivery mode)
951:openspec config profile core
952:```
953:
954:`openspec config profile` starts with a current-state summary, then lets you choose:
955:- Change delivery + workflows
956:- Change delivery only
957:- Change workflows only
958:- Keep current settings (exit)
959:
960:If you keep current settings, no changes are written and no update prompt is shown.
961:If there are no config changes but the current project files are out of sync with your global profile/delivery, OpenSpec will show a warning and suggest running `openspec update`.
962:Pressing `Ctrl+C` also cancels the flow cleanly (no stack trace) and exits with code `130`.
963:In the workflow checklist, `[x]` means the workflow is selected in global config. To apply those selections to project files, run `openspec update` (or choose `Apply changes to this project now?` when prompted inside a project).
964:
965:**Interactive examples:**
966:
967:```bash
968:# Delivery-only update
969:openspec config profile
970:# choose: Change delivery only
971:# choose delivery: Skills only
972:
973:# Workflows-only update
974:openspec config profile
975:# choose: Change workflows only
976:# toggle workflows in the checklist, then confirm
977:```

1059:## Environment Variables
1060:
1061:| Variable | Description |
1062:|----------|-------------|
1063:| `OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY` | Set to `0` to disable telemetry |
1064:| `DO_NOT_TRACK` | Set to `1` to disable telemetry (standard DNT signal) |
1065:| `OPENSPEC_CONCURRENCY` | Default concurrency for bulk validation (default: 6) |
1066:| `EDITOR` or `VISUAL` | Editor for `openspec config edit` |
1067:| `NO_COLOR` | Disable color output when set |

36:/**
37: * Registry for looking up tool command adapters.
38: */
39:export class CommandAdapterRegistry {
40:  private static adapters: Map<string, ToolCommandAdapter> = new Map();
41:
42:  // Static initializer - register built-in adapters
43:  static {
44:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(amazonQAdapter);
45:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(antigravityAdapter);
46:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(auggieAdapter);
47:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(bobAdapter);
48:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(claudeAdapter);
49:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(clineAdapter);
50:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(codexAdapter);
51:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(codebuddyAdapter);
52:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(continueAdapter);
53:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(costrictAdapter);
54:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(crushAdapter);
55:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(cursorAdapter);
56:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(factoryAdapter);
57:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(geminiAdapter);
58:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(githubCopilotAdapter);
59:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(iflowAdapter);
60:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(junieAdapter);
61:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(kilocodeAdapter);
62:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(kiroAdapter);
63:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(opencodeAdapter);
64:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(piAdapter);
65:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(qoderAdapter);
66:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(lingmaAdapter);
67:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(qwenAdapter);
68:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(roocodeAdapter);
69:    CommandAdapterRegistry.register(windsurfAdapter);
70:  }
71:
72:  /**
73:   * Register a tool command adapter.
74:   * @param adapter - The adapter to register
75:   */
76:  static register(adapter: ToolCommandAdapter): void {
77:    CommandAdapterRegistry.adapters.set(adapter.toolId, adapter);
78:  }
79:
80:  /**
81:   * Get an adapter by tool ID.
82:   * @param toolId - The tool identifier (e.g., 'claude', 'cursor')
83:   * @returns The adapter or undefined if not registered
84:   */
85:  static get(toolId: string): ToolCommandAdapter | undefined {
86:    return CommandAdapterRegistry.adapters.get(toolId);
87:  }
88:
89:  /**
90:   * Get all registered adapters.
91:   * @returns Array of all registered adapters
92:   */
93:  static getAll(): ToolCommandAdapter[] {
94:    return Array.from(CommandAdapterRegistry.adapters.values());
95:  }
96:
97:  /**
98:   * Check if an adapter is registered for a tool.
99:   * @param toolId - The tool identifier
100:   * @returns True if an adapter exists
101:   */
102:  static has(toolId: string): boolean {
103:    return CommandAdapterRegistry.adapters.has(toolId);
104:  }

134:function validateFolderStyleName(name: string, label: string): string {
135:  if (name.length === 0) {
136:    throw new Error(`${label} must not be empty`);
137:  }
138:
139:  if (name === '.' || name === '..') {
140:    throw new Error(`${label} must not be '${name}'`);
141:  }
142:
143:  if (/[\\/]/u.test(name)) {
144:    throw new Error(`${label} must not contain path separators`);
145:  }
146:
147:  return name;
148:}
149:
150:export function validateWorkspaceName(name: string): string {
151:  validateFolderStyleName(name, 'Workspace name');
152:
153:  if (!/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/u.test(name)) {
154:    throw new Error(
155:      'Workspace name must be kebab-case with lowercase letters, numbers, and single hyphen separators'
156:    );
157:  }
158:
159:  return name;
160:}

163:  return validateFolderStyleName(name, 'Workspace link name');

184:async function pathIsFile(filePath: string): Promise<boolean> {
185:  try {
186:    return (await fs.stat(filePath)).isFile();
187:  } catch {
188:    return false;
189:  }
190:}

192:async function pathIsDirectory(dirPath: string): Promise<boolean> {
193:  try {
194:    return (await fs.stat(dirPath)).isDirectory();
195:  } catch {
196:    return false;
197:  }
198:}

201:  return pathIsFile(getWorkspaceSharedStatePath(candidateRoot));

204:async function getSearchStartDirectory(startPath: string): Promise<string> {
205:  const resolvedStart = path.resolve(startPath);
206:
207:  try {
208:    const stats = await fs.stat(resolvedStart);
209:    return stats.isDirectory() ? resolvedStart : path.dirname(resolvedStart);
210:  } catch {
211:    return resolvedStart;
212:  }
213:}
214:
215:export async function findWorkspaceRoot(startPath = process.cwd()): Promise<string | null> {
216:  let currentDir = await getSearchStartDirectory(startPath);
217:
218:  while (true) {
219:    if (await isWorkspaceRoot(currentDir)) {
220:      return process.platform === 'win32'
221:        ? FileSystemUtils.canonicalizeExistingPath(currentDir)
222:        : currentDir;
223:    }
224:
225:    const parentDir = path.dirname(currentDir);
226:    if (parentDir === currentDir) {
227:      return null;
228:    }
229:
230:    currentDir = parentDir;
231:  }
232:}

265:function formatZodIssues(error: z.ZodError): string {
266:  return error.issues
267:    .map((issue) => {
268:      const location = issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join('.') : 'root';
269:      return `${location}: ${issue.message}`;
270:    })

272:}

283:function assertValidMapKeys(
284:  keys: string[],
285:  validator: (name: string) => string,
286:  label: string
287:): void {
288:  for (const key of keys) {
289:    try {
290:      validator(key);
291:    } catch (error) {
292:      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
293:      throw new Error(`Invalid ${label} '${key}': ${message}`);
294:    }
295:  }
296:}
297:
298:function formatSupportedOpenerValues(): string {
299:  return WORKSPACE_SUPPORTED_OPENER_VALUES.join(', ');
300:}
301:
302:export function isWorkspaceAgentOpenerId(value: string): value is WorkspaceAgentOpenerId {
303:  return (WORKSPACE_AGENT_OPENER_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
304:}
305:
306:export function isWorkspaceSupportedOpenerValue(
307:  value: string
308:): value is WorkspaceSupportedOpenerValue {
309:  return (WORKSPACE_SUPPORTED_OPENER_VALUES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
310:}
311:
312:export function parseWorkspacePreferredOpenerValue(value: string): WorkspacePreferredOpener {
313:  if (value === 'editor') {
314:    return {
315:      kind: 'editor',
316:      id: 'vscode',
317:    };
318:  }
319:
320:  if (isWorkspaceAgentOpenerId(value)) {
321:    return {
322:      kind: 'agent',
323:      id: value,
324:    };
325:  }
326:
327:  throw new Error(
328:    `Unsupported workspace opener '${value}'. Supported values: ${formatSupportedOpenerValues()}`
329:  );
330:}
331:
332:export function validateWorkspacePreferredOpener(
333:  opener: WorkspacePreferredOpener
334:): WorkspacePreferredOpener {
335:  if (opener.kind === 'editor' && opener.id === 'vscode') {
336:    return opener;
337:  }
338:
339:  if (opener.kind === 'agent' && isWorkspaceAgentOpenerId(opener.id)) {
340:    return opener;
341:  }
342:
343:  throw new Error(
344:    `Unsupported workspace opener '${opener.kind}:${opener.id}'. Supported values: ${formatSupportedOpenerValues()}`
345:  );
346:}
347:
348:export function parseWorkspaceSharedState(content: string): WorkspaceSharedState {
349:  const raw = parseYamlObject(content, 'workspace shared state');
350:  const result = SharedStateSchema.safeParse(raw);
351:
352:  if (!result.success) {
353:    throw new Error(`Invalid workspace shared state: ${formatZodIssues(result.error)}`);
354:  }
355:
356:  validateWorkspaceName(result.data.name);
357:  assertValidMapKeys(
358:    Object.keys(result.data.links),
359:    validateWorkspaceLinkName,
360:    'workspace link name'
361:  );
362:
363:  return {
364:    version: 1,
365:    name: result.data.name,
366:    links: result.data.links,
367:  };
368:}
369:
370:export function parseWorkspaceLocalState(content: string): WorkspaceLocalState {
371:  const raw = parseYamlObject(content, 'workspace local state');
372:  const result = LocalStateSchema.safeParse(raw);
373:
374:  if (!result.success) {
375:    throw new Error(`Invalid workspace local state: ${formatZodIssues(result.error)}`);
376:  }
377:
378:  assertValidMapKeys(
379:    Object.keys(result.data.paths),
380:    validateWorkspaceLinkName,
381:    'workspace local path name'
382:  );
383:
384:  const preferredOpener = result.data.preferred_opener
385:    ? validateWorkspacePreferredOpener(result.data.preferred_opener as WorkspacePreferredOpener)
386:    : undefined;
387:
388:  return {
389:    version: 1,
390:    paths: result.data.paths,
391:    ...(preferredOpener ? { preferred_opener: preferredOpener } : {}),
392:  };
393:}
394:
395:export function parseWorkspaceRegistryState(content: string): WorkspaceRegistryState {
396:  const raw = parseYamlObject(content, 'workspace registry state');
397:  const result = RegistryStateSchema.safeParse(raw);
398:
399:  if (!result.success) {
400:    throw new Error(`Invalid workspace registry state: ${formatZodIssues(result.error)}`);
401:  }
402:
403:  assertValidMapKeys(
404:    Object.keys(result.data.workspaces),
405:    validateWorkspaceName,
406:    'workspace registry name'
407:  );
408:
409:  return {
410:    version: 1,
411:    workspaces: result.data.workspaces,
412:  };
413:}
414:
415:export function serializeWorkspaceSharedState(state: WorkspaceSharedState): string {
416:  validateWorkspaceName(state.name);
417:  assertValidMapKeys(Object.keys(state.links), validateWorkspaceLinkName, 'workspace link name');
418:
419:  for (const [linkName, linkState] of Object.entries(state.links)) {
420:    if (!isPlainObject(linkState)) {
421:      throw new Error(`Invalid workspace link '${linkName}': link state must be an object`);
422:    }
423:  }
424:
425:  return stringifyYaml({
426:    version: 1,
427:    name: state.name,
428:    links: state.links,
429:  });
430:}
431:
432:export function serializeWorkspaceLocalState(state: WorkspaceLocalState): string {
433:  assertValidMapKeys(
434:    Object.keys(state.paths),
435:    validateWorkspaceLinkName,
436:    'workspace local path name'
437:  );
438:
439:  for (const [linkName, localPath] of Object.entries(state.paths)) {
440:    if (typeof localPath !== 'string') {
441:      throw new Error(`Invalid workspace local path '${linkName}': path must be a string`);
442:    }
443:  }
444:
445:  const preferredOpener = state.preferred_opener
446:    ? validateWorkspacePreferredOpener(state.preferred_opener)
447:    : undefined;
448:
449:  return stringifyYaml({
450:    version: 1,
451:    paths: state.paths,
452:    ...(preferredOpener ? { preferred_opener: preferredOpener } : {}),
453:  });
454:}
455:
456:export function serializeWorkspaceRegistryState(state: WorkspaceRegistryState): string {
457:  assertValidMapKeys(
458:    Object.keys(state.workspaces),
459:    validateWorkspaceName,
460:    'workspace registry name'
461:  );
462:
463:  for (const [workspaceName, workspaceRoot] of Object.entries(state.workspaces)) {
464:    if (typeof workspaceRoot !== 'string') {
465:      throw new Error(`Invalid workspace registry entry '${workspaceName}': path must be a string`);
466:    }
467:  }
468:
469:  return stringifyYaml({
470:    version: 1,
471:    workspaces: state.workspaces,
472:  });
473:}
474:
475:export function listWorkspaceRegistryEntries(
476:  registry: WorkspaceRegistryState
477:): WorkspaceRegistryEntry[] {
478:  return Object.entries(registry.workspaces)
479:    .map(([name, workspaceRoot]) => ({ name, workspaceRoot }))
480:    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
481:}
482:
483:export async function readWorkspaceSharedState(workspaceRoot: string): Promise<WorkspaceSharedState> {
484:  return parseWorkspaceSharedState(
485:    await fs.readFile(getWorkspaceSharedStatePath(workspaceRoot), 'utf-8')
486:  );
487:}
488:
489:export async function readWorkspaceLocalState(workspaceRoot: string): Promise<WorkspaceLocalState> {
490:  return parseWorkspaceLocalState(
491:    await fs.readFile(getWorkspaceLocalStatePath(workspaceRoot), 'utf-8')
492:  );
493:}
494:
495:function isFileNotFoundError(error: unknown): boolean {
496:  return (
497:    typeof error === 'object' &&
498:    error !== null &&
499:    'code' in error &&
500:    (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT'
501:  );
502:}
503:
504:export async function readOptionalWorkspaceLocalState(
505:  workspaceRoot: string
506:): Promise<WorkspaceLocalState | null> {
507:  try {
508:    return await readWorkspaceLocalState(workspaceRoot);
509:  } catch (error) {
510:    if (isFileNotFoundError(error)) {
511:      return null;
512:    }
513:
514:    throw error;
515:  }
516:}
